`TOO_MANY_MAPPINGS`, `CONFLICTING_MAPPINGS`, `MAPPING_NOT_EFFECTIVE`, `INVALID_NOTE`; 503 `UNAVAILABLE {component}` (`features`, `sequence`, `nextactions`, `atlas`). Every message names ids and fields, never titles, rationales or Muse text (C144).

   ⇒ `pop` and `electronic` adjacent with `pop` first, `reggae` after), `TestSound_DigestIncludesComputeDef`, `TestSound_PrimaryValue`, `TestSubjectOf_Table`; the L1 sounds goldens that embed digests are re-baselined with a reviewed diff.

### C144. Privacy and visibility enforcement (extends L1 C99 and L2 C127)

1. **Muse text.** Allowlist (`TestMuseText_OnlyInAllowlistedOps`, the `museTextOps` variable of `internal/api/ops_l1_leaks_test.go`): L1's `{list_muses, get_muse, create_muse, update_muse, get_song, get_muse_board, get_track_context, get_draft}` + L2's `get_record` + **L3's `list_next_actions`**; the canary Muse sits on the fixture Songs and every one of the 133 operations runs over REST and MCP; `get_song_atlas`, `get_adherence`, `suggest_sequence`, `propose_taxonomy`, `list_taxonomy_proposals`, `decide_taxonomy_proposal`, `edit_record_slots` (with `sequence`), `propose_record` (with `sequence`) and `get_record include=sequence` must contain no canary byte (the Atlas `muses` dictionary is ids).
2. **Readers.** `TestMuseReaders_L3` (C133). `nextactions` reads Muse text only through `muses.Service.Refs`; it cannot reach `export*`, `notify`, `audit`, `backup`, `subsonic*`, `tape`, `albums` (import graph), and its texts are never written to a table, a log or an audit field (`TestNextActions_NotPersisted`: SQL scan for any `next_actions` text in `audit_log`, `idempotency`, `job_events`) **and never put into ntfy or any other notification text** (`record_proposed_waiting` is the likely temptation; the existing L2 notification carries a count only; `TestNotify_NoNextActionText` drives proposal events against the ntfy fake with canaries and asserts no next-action string is sent).
3. **Agent-authored text.** `taxonomy_proposals.rationale` and proposal `term`s containing Muse-looking text (canaries) are stored and returned by `list_taxonomy_proposals`/`get_sound_taxonomy` by design and appear nowhere else (logs, audit text, exports, ntfy fake, `problem+json`, `get_stats`); the canary run includes **`get_audit`**, which must show the L3 calls (operation, status, ids) and none of the rationale or term text.
4. **Errors and logs.** No title, rationale or Muse text in `Problem.Message`/`Details` (ids and field names only; `TestL3Ops_ErrorsCarryIDsOnly` drives every L3 error code with canaries), nor in `slog` attributes.
5. **Fixtures and docs.** Synthetic Muse names only (`Synthetic Muse NNN`); `TestFixtures_L3_SyntheticOnly`; docs use `mm.example.com` and invented titles; nothing in the public part of this plan names a real inspiration; `make gate` unchanged.
6. **No leakage through features.** Features, sequences and the Atlas contain numbers, ids and Song titles only; `TestFeatures_NoTextColumns` asserts the DDL of `take_features`/`song_features` has no free-text column except `detail_json` (numbers and codes only, golden-pinned).
7. **Song titles are never Muse text.** D-L1-8 forbids a Song title equal to or containing Muse text, and the Atlas, `suggest_sequence` and next actions now surface titles in bulk: `TestSongTitle_NeverMuseText` (canary Muse title and artist on the fixture Muses; assert by SQL that no Song or Record title equals or contains either, and that no title-bearing L3 response — Atlas points, `suggest_sequence` steps, `get_adherence` items, next-action `{title}` fields — contains a canary byte; `list_next_actions` `{muse}` positions are the only permitted occurrences).

### C145. Web (`web/`; amends M2a C40, M3 C84, L1 C100, L2 C131 and the UI redesign shell)

**Placement decision (D-L3-37).** The Atlas is a **Library sub-view**: the same job as Takes, Sounds, Records and Crates (browse the whole library, then hand a selection to a Crate or a Record), so it joins the Library tab's `SubNav` row, which reads **Takes · Sounds · Atlas · Records · Crates · Playlists** once L2 rev 3 has added Records and Crates (the row already scrolls
horizontally). The **Sequence** panel is a tab of the Record studio because sequencing is an edit of one Record; **Proposals** is a tab of `/sounds` because a proposal is a taxonomy act; **What next** is a section of the **New** landing page, where the operator's pending decisions and an agent's open work already meet. No new tab, **no new secure page** (`decide_taxonomy_proposal` is an in-app
act on `/api/` like `pin_pick`), no new icon unless a lane needs one (`SearchIcon`, `StarIcon`, `UpIcon`, `DownIcon`, `CloseIcon` exist), `playerStore` and `player.svelte.ts` untouched (the Atlas plays nothing: a point or row opens `/songs/:id`, which plays through the player).

**C145.1 Shell integration (F3 owns these files; nobody else edits them).**
- `lib/router.ts` `APP_ROUTES` (specific before parametric): `/atlas` → `atlas`, placed after `/sounds`. It is an SPA route (no server prefix).
- `lib/nav.ts`: `TAB_OF.atlas = "library"`; `NAV_LINKS` gains `{Atlas, /atlas, library}` directly after Sounds; `subActive` gains the `/atlas` case; `TITLES.atlas = "Atlas"`; `isTabRoot` gains `atlas`. `CONTEXTUAL_ROUTES` is unchanged (the Atlas is reached from the row). `navTabs.test.ts` (one tab per route, every `NAV_LINKS` path routable) and `ux-shell.spec.ts` (walks `NAV_LINKS`) are the guards.
- `lib/scrollRestore.ts`: `READY_ROUTES` gains `atlas`; the route calls `routeReady()` once its first page has loaded (the router owns scroll; a route never calls `scrollTo` itself).
- `App.svelte`: one `{:else if}` branch for `atlas`; F3 ships a **placeholder** `routes/Atlas.svelte` (a heading and a "not built yet" line) so WM2 fills a file it owns.
- Every other L3 page change is a **named edit of an existing route file with exactly one owner** (the table in "File ownership"): `Record.svelte` (WS), `Sounds.svelte` and `New.svelte` (WA1), `Song.svelte` and `Muse.svelte` (WA2), `Board.svelte` (WM2).
- Rules for every L3 file: Svelte 5 runes only; no `fetch` outside `web/src/api/` (the ESLint ban stays); every interactive element is at least `--hit` (44 px) on touch (`smallTargets` check in the specs); **dark only** (no theme switch, no `prefers-color-scheme` branch); colours only from `styles/tokens.css`, no colour literal elsewhere (the Atlas categorical palette is a table of CSS custom properties added to `tokens.css` by WM1, the one edit of that file); motion respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

**C145.2 Atlas (`routes/Atlas.svelte`; page WM2, canvas and view model WM1).** URL state `?x=&y=&color=&dim=&muse=&session=&state=&sound=facet:value&q=&crate=&near=` (repeated keys read from the raw search string as Library/Board do). Layout: toolbar — **preset buttons** (Tempo × Energy, Family × Vocal, Requested × Got), x/y axis selects (an equal pair is refused client-side with the
server's `AXIS_PAIR_INVALID` text), **Colour by** select, **Dim non-matching** toggle, **Hide predicted points** toggle (client-side: basis `p` hidden; default ON until the D-L3-14 box is ticked), the Board's filter controls (session select, state toggles, facet pills from `search_sounds(limit=0)`, Muse picker, Crate select, search box); a **totals strip** from `totals`
("443 plotted · 13 measured · 430 estimated · 26 not plotted"); the **canvas**; a **legend** (counts; clicking an entry dims the others client-side) with a footnote: "Predicted energy is estimated from tempo band, genre and mood, so unrendered Songs form stacks; it is not a measurement."; a **selection bar**; in-page tabs (a segmented control, not routes) **Map** and **Requested vs got**
(`get_adherence`: summary line, by-facet table, items table with links to `/songs/:id`).
*Canvas (`AtlasCanvas.svelte`):* a `<canvas>` sized to its container with `devicePixelRatio`; axes with ticks (tempo: 60, 80, 100, 120, 160, 200 on the log scale; energy 0–1; categorical band labels); points are circles — **solid** for measured/declared/plan, **hollow** for `predicted` on either plotted axis; selected points get a ring; for `requested-got` the guide lines (identity, octave up,
octave down) are drawn; hover and keyboard focus show a tooltip (title, state, Pick source, tempo/energy/key with their basis words, the `subject_note`); wheel/pinch zoom 1×–16× and drag pan; **rectangle select** (shift-drag, or the **Select** mode toggle on touch — the Board's `selectMode` pattern); the hit radius is at least 22 px on touch so a point is a 44 px target; keyboard — the canvas is focusable,
arrow keys move the focus to the nearest point in that direction (grid index), Enter opens the Song, Space toggles selection, Escape clears; a **Show as table** button swaps the canvas for a paged table of the same points (title, x, y, basis, state, Pick, colour value; rows ≥ 44 px) so screen readers and tests read the same data. The canvas never takes keyboard focus away from the global player shortcuts without an explicit focus.
*Selection bar* (D-L3-26): count; **Add to Crate…** (L2's `AddToCrate`, subject `song`, `edit_crate_items`); **New Record from selection…** (`create_record{song_ids}` ≤ 60 with the selection capped and a note beyond 60, then `router.navigate("/records/:id?sequence=arc")`, which opens the Sequence tab with the preview); **Open** (one selected ⇒ `/songs/:id`); **Show on the Board**
(`/board?…` with the selected Songs' Muse when all share one). On a phone the bar is a bottom sheet **above** the MiniPlayer (it offsets by `--footer-h` and `--nav-b`, never covers the tab bar) and a route change dismisses it (the shell's sheet rule).
*Loading:* follows `next_cursor` until done or `MAX_VIEW = 5 000` points; a progress note; `pending: true` ⇒ one refetch after 1.5 s, at most three times; a 409 `ATLAS_STALE` restarts from the first page (debounced; "Map changed — reload" when it repeats). The route calls `routeReady()` after the first page.

**C145.3 Sequence tab (Record studio `routes/Record.svelte`, `SequencePanel.svelte`; WS).** The studio's in-page segmented control (Slots · Changelog · Liner notes · Energy · Tapes) gains **Sequence**. Strategy select (arc, ramp up, steady), optional **Start with** / **End with** (the Record's Songs), **Preview** ⇒ `suggest_sequence(record_id, …)`: a table of steps (position, Song, the rendered sentence,
tempo/key/energy chips with basis markers), a cost bar (input → output, improvement %), coverage chips ("tempo 6/6 · key 4/6 · energy 6/6 (2 predicted)"), warnings in words; **Apply** ⇒ `edit_record_slots{revision, ops: [{op: "sequence", …}]}` (the studio's usual `revision` conflict banner and `pending` refetch); the tab also shows `get_record include=sequence` — the latest explanation,
who applied it and when, and an `Order changed since` badge when `stale`. The Propose dialog gains **Sequence before proposing** (strategy select) ⇒ `propose_record{…, sequence}`. `?sequence=<strategy>` opens the tab and runs the preview. Edits on a published Record by a non-human viewer are not offered (L2's `canEditPublished`). The studio's changelog renders the three new `ChangeText` rows through the mirror in `recordView.ts`.

**C145.4 Proposals tab (`/sounds`, `TaxonomyProposals.svelte`; WA1).** The Sounds tablist (Songs · Terms) gains **Proposals** (`sel.tab = "proposals"`, URL state like the others). `list_taxonomy_proposals(state=open)` by default (+ history filter). Per proposal: the agent, the rationale (text node), a **mappings table** with a checkbox per mapping (the `op`, `term`, `facet`, `value`), the **impact preview**
("14 Songs gain *reggae*, 2 lose *pop*; clears the unmapped term *skank* in 14 Songs", `include_impact` refetch button when old), buttons **Accept selected**, **Accept all**, **Reject** (optional note) ⇒ `decide_taxonomy_proposal`; the result states what was applied (`version`, recomputed counts) and what was skipped as stale; a 409 `TAXONOMY_STALE` shows "nothing left to apply — proposal expired".
Viewers that are not human see the list read-only. The Terms tab's "Map…" remains for humans.

**C145.5 New: "What next" (`NextActions.svelte`; WA1).** A section of `routes/New.svelte` directly below "Needs you": `list_next_actions(limit=20)` for the viewer: cards grouped by code with the `more` count ("{More} more like this"); each card's hint becomes a link through `nextActionsView.hintLink`: `get_muse` ⇒ `/muses/:id`, `get_song` ⇒ `/songs/:id`, `get_record` ⇒ `/records/:id`,
`suggest_sequence` ⇒ `/records/:id?sequence=arc`, `list_songs` with a Muse ⇒ `/board?muse=…`, `estimate_generation` (from_draft) ⇒ a **draftsIntent** to `/human/generate` (the M3 full-page estimate in the in-app secure route; **never** an `/api/` create), `create_duel` ⇒ the Song page, `propose_taxonomy`/`get_sound_taxonomy` ⇒ `/sounds`.
Human-audience items are labelled "needs you". The section is collapsed to a count when empty and never blocks the page (a failure keeps the previous list). Nothing on it spends or starts anything. **Needs-you counts (D-L3-33):** `inbox.svelte.ts`/`inbox.ts` extend `needsYouCount()` with proposed Records (`list_records?status=proposed&limit=1`) and open taxonomy proposals
(`list_taxonomy_proposals?state=open&limit=1`), each failure keeping the previous number; the "Needs you" section prints them with links (the Records list's *proposed* chip, the `/sounds` Proposals tab).

**C145.6 Entry points on existing pages (named edits, one owner each).** **Song** (WA2): the features strip (`TempoBadge` from L1 plus `KeyBadge`, `EnergyBadge`), "Show on the Atlas" (`/atlas?near=<id>` is the demoted Nearby, D-L3-35), **Nearby** (`get_song_atlas near_song_id`, k = 8, a cut candidate); **Muse** (WA2): the Muse's next actions (`list_next_actions subject_kind=muse`);
**Board** (WM2, `Board.svelte` and `boardView.ts`): **View on the Atlas** in the toolbar (same filter query string). The nav needs no new badge: the New tab badge already sums needs-you counts (C145.5).

**C145.7 View-model modules (`web/src/lib/`, each with a bun test).** `atlasView.ts` (axis → screen transforms, tick generation incl. log ticks, spatial-grid hit test, nearest-in-direction, rectangle selection, legend palette, filter ⇄ query string, basis → marker), `palette.ts` (a 12-colour categorical palette + "other" built from the tokens;
`palette.test.ts` asserts 12 distinct colours and **≥ 3:1 contrast against the dark `--bg` and `--surface` tokens it reads from `tokens.css`**, computed; there is no light theme), `sequenceView.ts` (preview table model, strategy labels, endpoint options, the `sequence` op builder with the revision), `proposalView.ts` (impact text, subset ⇒ `accept_mappings`, result text),
`nextActionsView.ts` (grouping, `hintLink`), `featuresView.ts` (basis labels, `KeyBadge`/`EnergyBadge` text), `atlasApi.ts` (typed wrappers over the generated client, paging helper, stale-restart), and the inbox additions (`inbox.test.ts` extended). `recordView.ts` (L2's file) gains the three `changeText` rows (WS).

**C145.8 Stable test ids.** `atlas-canvas`, `atlas-table`, `atlas-preset-<name>`, `atlas-totals`, `atlas-legend`, `atlas-select-bar`, `atlas-tab-<name>`, `sequence-panel`, `sequence-preview`, `sequence-apply`, `sequence-stale`, `proposal-card`, `proposal-accept`, `proposal-reject`, `next-actions`, `next-action-card`, `key-badge`, `energy-badge`; the Library `SubNav` link is found by role and name.

### C146. Tooling, fixtures, CI, performance, docs (amends M2a C41/C42, M3 C85/C86, L1 C101, M4a C116/C117, L2 C132)

**C146.0 The agent loop (binding for every lane; `CLAUDE.md` in the worktree is authoritative; D-L3-43).** An agent iterates with **`make verify`** (only what its checkout changed versus `origin/main`: gofmt, vet, lint, `go test -race -short` over the changed packages and their reverse dependencies, web checks if `web/` changed, the openapi drift check, the incremental `make gate`,
and the Playwright specs mapped from the changed files by `scripts/e2e-map.txt`), plus **focused package tests** (`go test ./internal/sequencer/ -run TestPlan`; a single-package `-race` run is allowed; any other `-race` run and every Playwright run goes through the lock-taking shims **`scripts/go-test`** and **`scripts/pw`**; whole-repo suites only through `make`). A lane runs **only the Playwright specs
its change affects** (`MM_E2E_SPECS` / the `make verify` mapping), never the whole matrix. **The full `make test`, `make test-long` (only if jobs/acceptance/`TestAcceptance_` packages were touched), `make e2e` and `make perf` are run once by the integration lanes (INT-B, INT-W) or left to GitHub CI** (6 Go race shards balanced by `scripts/ci-shard-weights.txt`, the perf job, 6 e2e shards,
the coverage job with `served-check`, the history gate). Every lane ends with `make gate` green (incremental; CI and the pre-push hook run `gate-full`), `make openapi` producing **no diff** (the API surface is frozen after F2), and its PR description listing the plan-document addenda X1 must apply and the weights of any new package slower than 15 s. Every command goes through `make`/`scripts/loop`
(the metrics experiment); nothing here bypasses the wrapper.

**C146.1 Fixtures.** `librarytest.FixtureL3(tb, st, now)` / `ApplyL3` on top of `FixtureL2` (which tests `Settle` first) — **SQL-only, written by F1** (no services; the Reconciler derives Songs, Picks and Sound rows when a test calls `Settle`; all names synthetic), **plus the literal `take_features`/`song_features` rows** of the Songs below (computed once by an independent calculation from the tables of C135 and pinned as literals, so the `features` lanes have an oracle that shares no code with `DeriveTake`; where L2's fixture creates Muses and Songs directly in SQL, F1 follows the same technique after re-reading it). Contents: Muse **M5** with Songs **T1–T8** (T1–T6 are the *strong* set, T7 and T8 below) — one take each, human stars `5,4,5,4,5,4`, `analyses` rows `tempo` (96.0, 98.0, 100.0, 104.0, 108.0, 102.0 BPM), `key` (8A, 8A, 9A, 9A, 9B, 8B), `energy` (0.30, 0.45, 0.55, 0.70, 0.90, 0.40), `loudness` — i.e. exactly the table of C137.6; **T7** (3★) and **T8** (unrated), each with one take and the same kinds of analyses; the styles of T1–T8 declare the same BPM as their measured tempo (no tempo mismatch anywhere); Muse **M3** with three draft Songs **d1–d3** (angles `remake-long`, `alt-take-long`, `long`; styles with a BPM term, a mood and a genre); Muse **M4** with **u1** (one take, unrated) and the draft Song **u2**; a Muse-less draft Song **d4**; the term `skank` in the styles of `d1, d2, d3, u2, d4` (so five Songs carry it unmapped). No Record contains T1–T8. `FixtureL3Audio(tb, st, data)` (E1, **media tier**) renders real audio with the existing synth: 24 triad progressions (one per key, 60 s, A4 = 440 Hz), the same 24 detuned by −35 cents, three click tracks (70, 96, 128 BPM), a dense-bright track, a sine pad and noise, with matching loudness analyses. `FixtureL3Mirror(tb, st, now, params)` (INT-B) builds the acceptance mirror on `FixtureL2Mirror` (L1/L2 parameters `Muses 120, DemoDrafts 5, Originals 4, Groups 5`, unlike the real library) and writes analyses for a deterministic subset (`Mono` fakes: the four originals and eight rendered Songs get measured tempo, key and energy). `FixtureL3Scale(tb, st, now, songs, muses)` (F1) writes rows only (no audio; feature rows included) for performance tests (`songs: 5 000`, `Muses: 200`). `FixtureL3Upgrade(tb, st, now)` (F1) builds the data at schema 10 for `TestMigration0011_UpgradeFromL2`. Existing fixtures keep their row counts; `TestFixtures_L3_SyntheticOnly`.
**Expected after `Settle` on `FixtureL3` (literals in `TestAcceptance_L3_FixtureCounts`, computed once by independent SQL over source tables):** takes 17 (8 from L2 + T1–T8 + u1), Songs 21 (7 + 8 + 3 + 2 + 1), `take_features` 17, `song_features` 21; T1–T8 have tempo basis `measured` and energy basis `measured` and key basis `measured`; the draft Songs d1–d3, u2, d4 have tempo basis `declared`, energy basis `predicted`, key NULL; `list_next_actions` for an agent with generate scope returns, by `(code, subject)` and in this order within equal priority: `record_undecided_slots` (R1, R2), `muse_record_candidate` (M5), `muse_unrendered` (M3), `song_duel` (S3, S4), `muse_unjudged` (M1, M4), `muse_partial_angles` (M4), `record_empty_slot` (R1 slot D), `taxonomy_unmapped` (`skank`, 5 Songs), `song_no_muse` (S6 only **if its recipe is known**, otherwise none; u1 has M4, T1–T8 have M5, S7 and d4 have no take; NA3 pins the literal from the fixture), and **no** `record_proposed_waiting` or `taxonomy_proposals_open` (human audience, none yet); the exact texts are pinned by `testdata/next_actions_fixture.golden.json`. A **human** principal additionally sees `features_missing` (one library-level item: 7 Songs whose subject take has no measured tempo, key or energy — S1–S6 and u1); an agent never does (no analyze operation exists for it).

**C146.2 Rigs.** `servertest.Options` gains `L3 bool` (adds `FixtureL3`; requires `L2`) and `RealFeatures` (media tier: the production decode and analyzers; default is the `earstest` fakes). `Rig.Settle` already runs `EnsureSeed`, the provenance `Settle` (which now includes the features step once INT-B wires the real one) and `Sweep`, then L2's Syncer.
The parity runner gets **six** `ParityCase` rows, one lane-owned file each (created empty by F2): `internal/mcpgen/parity_l3_seq_test.go` (SR: `suggest_sequence`), `parity_l3_nextactions_test.go` (NA1: `list_next_actions`), `parity_l3_taxonomy_test.go` (TP2: `propose_taxonomy`, `list_taxonomy_proposals`), `parity_l3_atlas_test.go` (AT2: `get_song_atlas`),
`parity_l3_adherence_test.go` (AD: `get_adherence`); the aggregator `parity_l3_test.go` is F2's. **Only if D-L3-22 is approved** the `map_sound_term` row of `parity_l1_test.go` is removed (INT-B, one named edit). The extended `propose_record`/`edit_record_slots` arguments are new rows in `parity_l3_seq_test.go` (`TestParity_L3SequenceArgs`); L2's parity file is not edited.
`decide_taxonomy_proposal` and (with the box) `map_sound_term` have REST-only tests (`TestDecide_*`, `TestMap_AgentRefused`, `TestOps_HumanJudgment_NoMCP_L3`); the every-UI-action script (`internal/acceptance/every_ui_action_test.go`) gets entries for the **five** ops that have a UI route **and** an MCP face and, only with D-L3-22, drops `map_sound_term` (INT-B).

**C146.3 CI and coverage (I1).** There are no `go-fast`/`go-media` tiers: every new package lands in one of the 6 Go shards (`scripts/ci-go-test.sh`, weights in `scripts/ci-shard-weights.txt`; a package slower than 15 s needs a weights line, CI fails without one), the media tier runs under `MM_REQUIRE_MEDIA_TOOLS=1` (the key/energy/`Mono` tests and `FixtureL3Audio` need no special list), `TestPerf_*` run in the CI `perf` job
(also `make perf`), Playwright runs in 6 e2e shards with the `// matrix:` placement of each spec (C146.6), the `coverage` job merges unit + e2e coverage and runs `served-check`. `coverage.thresholds` gains: `internal/features`, `internal/sequencer` ≥ 95; `internal/sequence`, `internal/nextactions`, `internal/atlas` ≥ 90; the existing floors for `internal/analysis`, `internal/ears`, `internal/records`, `internal/sounds`
are unchanged; a new `internal/archtest/coverage_thresholds_l3_test.go` asserts the file names every L3 package. `.github/workflows/weekly.yml` gains **`features-property`** modelled on `provenance-property` (`MM_PROPERTY_SEQS: "300"`; the test reads `os.Getenv`, default 60) — a scope-valve cut candidate (D-L3-35).

**C146.4 Performance targets** (asserted through the env-scaled budget helper — `MM_PERF_FACTOR`, default 1, ×3 under `-race`; never a hard-coded wall-clock literal): `TestPerf_AtlasGet` — 5 000 Songs (`FixtureL3Scale`), tempo × energy, first page cold p95 over 20 runs **< 300 ms** (target 120 ms), warm pages 2–9 **< 80 ms** (scope query and token hash on every page, layout from the token-keyed cache); `TestPerf_AtlasLayout` — 10 000 points **< 150 ms**; `TestPerf_Features_Settle` — scale fixture first `Settle` of the features step **< 5 s** for 5 000 Songs, second **< 50 ms**; `TestPerf_NextActions` — 200 Muses × 3 Songs, 50 Records, `List(limit 20)` p95 **< 200 ms**, `ForTake` **< 30 ms**; `TestPerf_Sequencer` (C137.6); `TestPerf_Suggest` — a 60-slot Record end to end **< 150 ms**; `TestPerf_Impact` — 10 000 Songs **< 1.5 s**; `TestPerf_Key` (< 3 s CPU / 240 s of audio), `TestPerf_Energy` (< 1 s); a bun test builds the 600-point Atlas view model, projects it and builds the hit-test grid in **< 20 ms**, and a 5 000-point projection in **< 80 ms**.

**C146.5 Docs.** `docs/architecture.md` gains "Features, sequencing and the Atlas" (the four kinds of grouping table of "Why this model", the basis rule, the sequencer objective in words, the Atlas axes and honesty layer, the propose/decide table); `docs/runbook.md` gains "Analyse the library (key, energy, tempo)", "Taxonomy proposals", "Reading the Atlas totals", "After a restore (features rebuild)"; `docs/device-checklist.md` gains DC-L3-1 (Atlas on a phone: pan, pinch, rectangle select with the Select toggle, ≥ 44 px targets) and DC-L3-2 (play an agent-sequenced Record and judge the order by ear). The public plan copy `docs/plans/<date>-l3-library-agents-atlas.md` is the text above the private marker (X1, `scripts/l1-public-copy.sh` reused; lanes list the sections in their PR text, only X1 edits docs).

**C146.6 e2e conventions for L3 specs (INT-W writes them; lanes never add a spec file).** Every `web/e2e/l3-*.spec.ts` imports `test`/`expect` from `./fixtures` (never `@playwright/test`), starts with a `// matrix:` header and creates and resets its own data through an API helper `web/e2e/l3Api.ts` (the worker server is shared by the spec file only). Placement:
`l3-atlas` — `@mobile @firefox because the canvas, its touch selection and rectangle mode differ per engine and the selection sheet is a phone layout`; `l3-sequence` — `@mobile because the Record studio and its tabs are the phone layout`; `l3-next` — `@mobile because New is the PWA landing page`; `l3-taxonomy` — `chrome-only because it is a pure data flow`.
**No spec injects `analyses` rows** (no seed route exists, D-L3-45): draft Songs supply declared tempo and predicted energy; composed Songs get measured values only if the e2e server's Tier A runs on the fake provider's audio, and a spec then asserts *presence and basis*, never numbers (INT-W confirms this in its first commit; if Tier A is off in e2e, the specs use drafts only, which is enough for the Atlas,
the sequence preview and next actions). `scripts/e2e-map.txt` (I1) maps `web/src/routes/Atlas.svelte`, `web/src/lib/components/{Atlas*,Adherence*}`, `web/src/lib/{atlasView,palette,atlasApi}.ts` to `l3-atlas`; `routes/Record.svelte`, `components/SequencePanel.svelte`, `lib/sequenceView.ts` to `l3-sequence` (in addition to L2's mapping); `routes/{Sounds,New}.svelte`, `components/{TaxonomyProposals,NextActions}.svelte`,
`lib/{proposalView,nextActionsView,inbox*}.ts` to `l3-taxonomy`/`l3-next`; `served-check` requires the **six** L3 ops with a UI route (`suggest_sequence`, `list_next_actions`, `list_taxonomy_proposals`, `decide_taxonomy_proposal`, `get_song_atlas`, `get_adherence`) to be served to the SPA by some shard.

## Acceptance mapping (concept §7 L3 and design items → named tests)

---

## Lanes and tasks

Revision 2 cuts the work into **lanes**: each lane is one agent in its own git worktree (`../metamusic-wt/<lane>`, branch `l3-<lane>`), one reviewable PR (target **< ~2 500 changed lines excluding generated `openapi.json`/`schema.d.ts`/goldens**), and owns a closed set of files. Lanes iterate with `make verify` and focused package tests (C146.0);
the integration lanes run the whole suites. Sizes are planning estimates; a lane that outgrows 2.5k splits along its test list, not mid-file.

### The order

```
Stage 0 (serial, each lands on main before the next starts; needs L2 rev 3 merged and the two F1 boxes ticked)
   F1  migration 0011 + sounds amendment (ord, songDigest, SubjectOf) + guard tests + SQL fixtures (with literal feature rows)
   F2  contract freeze: types, 7 registrations with 501 stubs, frozen method sets and hooks (provenance, trackctx, records, sounds, ears), wiring, rigs, ceiling, openapi/schema.d.ts
   F3  web scaffold: /atlas route, nav, scroll-ready, placeholder page                                   (needs only F2's schema.d.ts)
Stage 1 (all parallel; backend lanes start when F2 has merged, web lanes when F3 has merged; the arrows are the only ordering)
   FC1 features pure functions          -> FC2 features service, Reconciler step, Verify, CLI
   E1  Ears: key, energy, shared decode (separable, D-L3-35)
   SQ1 sequencer cost model, explanation -> SQ2 planner (exact, beam, local)          -> SR  sequencing in Records, suggest_sequence, ops
   NA1 next-action engine, trackctx assembly, list_next_actions, first rules -> NA2 core rules, record/song rules -> NA3 demotable rules
   TP1 sounds Map split, hook, impact, propose/list      -> TP2 decide, ops, gated map_sound_term guard
   AT1 atlas axes and layout (pure)      -> AT2 atlas query, legend, neighbours, op
   AD  adherence + get_song.features (independent of the map)
   I1  CI, coverage, weekly job, e2e-map, scripts
   WM1 Atlas canvas and view model       -> WM2 Atlas page, adherence tab, Board entry
   WS  Sequence tab        WA1 Proposals tab, What next, needs-you counts        WA2 Song/Muse features and next actions     (web lanes; need F3)
Stage 3 (after every feature lane)   INT-B backend wiring + acceptance + leaks + parity     INT-W Playwright specs     (parallel)
Stage 4   X1 integration/public copy/docs/review     X2 production-copy check (operator-assisted)     X3 operator review
```

| **INT-B** | final wiring, acceptance, leaks, parity, property, restore | `internal/app/l3*.go` (bodies: the real `FeatureStep`, the recompute hook), `internal/acceptance/*_l3*_test.go`, `internal/api/ops_l3_leaks_test.go`, one-line named edits of `ops_l1_leaks_test.go`, `acceptance/every_ui_action_test.go` and (with D-L3-22) `mcpgen/parity_l1_test.go`, `internal/library/librarytest/fixture_l3_mirror*.go`, `internal/servertest/**` adjustments | all backend lanes | ~2 400 |
| **INT-W** | Playwright specs | `web/e2e/{l3-*.spec.ts,l3Api.ts}` | all web lanes + INT-B | ~1 200 |
| **X1–X3** | integration, public copy, docs, review; production-copy check; operator review | `docs/**`, `docs/plans/*`, the M5/M6 draft headers, release | all | small |

### File ownership and hot-spot rules

A file named under two lanes is a plan bug. **Files owned by F1/F2/F3 stay theirs**: a needed change goes back through this document and a fix commit on `main` that every lane rebases onto (small `fix(l3-freeze): …` PRs; the author is the owner of the file).

| Hot spot | Single owner | Rule for everyone else |
|---|---|---|
| `internal/api/api.go` (`New`, `Deps`), `internal/app/app.go`, `cmd/metamusic/{cmd_serve,cmd_library}.go` | F2 | F2 adds five `registerXxxL3` calls, the `Deps` fields, one call into `app/l3.go`, and the library hooks in `cmd/metamusic/l3_hooks.go`. Lanes fill bodies **only** in their own `app/l3_<lane>.go` (F2 creates them as empty functions: `l3_features` FC2, `l3_seq` SR, `l3_nextactions` NA1, `l3_taxonomy` TP2, `l3_atlas` AT2) and `cmd/metamusic/l3_cli.go` (FC2) |
| API registration (`internal/api/ops_*_l3.go`) | the lane in the table (sequence SR, nextactions NA1, taxonomy TP2, atlas AT2, adherence AD) | F2 writes the registrations with final types and `501 NOT_IMPLEMENTED` stub handlers; the owner replaces handler bodies only — never `Meta`, path, types or `operationId` |
| `edit_record_slots` / `propose_record` / `get_record` handlers (`ops_records_l2.go`, L2's file) | SR (named branches) | F2 changes only types and `Meta`; no other lane edits the file |
| `openapi.json`, `web/src/api/schema.d.ts` | F2 (frozen afterwards) | `make openapi` must show **no diff** in a lane; if the API surface must change, stop and raise a freeze fix. At rebase a conflict in these generated files is resolved by `git checkout origin/main -- <file>` then `make openapi`, never by hand |
| Op-count pins (`ops_m3_test.go`, `ops_m2c_test.go`), `toolsCeilingBytes`, `tools_list.golden.json` | F2 | untouched afterwards |
| `internal/store/migrations/**` | F1 | **no other lane adds a migration** (a column a lane thinks it needs is a freeze fix) |
| `internal/sounds/*` | by file: F1 `compute.go`, `subject.go`; F2 `service.go` (hook field/setter, `Changes.SongIDs`); TP1 `map.go`, `impact.go`, `proposals.go`, `proposals_list.go`; TP2 `proposals_decide.go` | no two lanes edit one file; every other `sounds` file is untouched by L3 |
| `internal/provenance/*` | F2 | FC2 and INT-B only supply the step behind the interface |
| `internal/trackctx/trackctx.go` | F2 (the perception key order, one call site) | `trackctx/nextactions.go` (the C139.4 assembly) is NA1's; no other lane edits `trackctx` |
| `internal/ears/types.go`, `analysis/blindspots.go` | F2 (Perception fields) / E1 | E1 owns every other file of `ears`, `analysis`, `analysis/ffmpeg` |
| `internal/records/*` (L2 files) | F2 (named hook sites: `SlotOp` fields and the dispatch in the slot-op files, the propose function), SR (`sequence*.go`, `text.go` rows) | L2's own tests stay green (`TestProposeRecord_L2Unchanged`) |
| `internal/recordview/*` | F2 (`service.go`), SR (`sequence.go`) | |
| `internal/songview/get.go` | AD (one call to `featuresBlock`) | `features.go` is AD's |
| `internal/nextactions/rules*.go` | NA1 (`rules.go`, `rules_a.go`, empty `rules_b.go`/`rules_c.go`), NA2 (`rules_b.go`), NA3 (`rules_c.go`) | the registry is `slices.Concat` of the three slices; the goldens are three files (`rules_v1_{a,b,c}.golden.json`) that `TestNextActions_RulesGolden` concatenates, so no file is edited by two lanes |
| `web/src/App.svelte`, `lib/router.ts`, `lib/nav.ts`, `lib/scrollRestore.ts` | F3 | web lanes fill the placeholder; no route, nav or `READY_ROUTES` change outside F3 |
| `web/src/styles/tokens.css` | WM1 (palette variables) | `tokens.test.ts` is the guard |
| Route files | `Atlas`, `Board`: WM2; `Record`: WS; `Sounds`, `New`, `inbox*`: WA1; `Song`, `Muse`: WA2 | named edits only; a route file never has two owners |
| `web/src/lib/recordView.ts` and its golden | WS (`changeText` rows) / SR (the Go test writes the golden) | WS's last commit follows SR's merge |
| `internal/api/ops_l1_leaks_test.go` (`museTextOps`), `acceptance/every_ui_action_test.go`, `mcpgen/parity_l1_test.go` | INT-B (one-line named edits) | lanes list the needed edit in their PR |
| `scripts/e2e-map.txt`, `scripts/ci-shard-weights.txt`, `coverage.thresholds`, `.github/**`, `Makefile` | I1 | lanes report needed mapping/weights in their PR text |
| `docs/architecture.md`, `docs/runbook.md`, `docs/device-checklist.md`, `docs/plans/**` | X1 | lanes list the sections/addenda to write in their PR text; no lane edits docs |
| `web/e2e/**` | INT-W | lanes write bun tests, never a Playwright spec |
| `internal/archtest/*_l3_test.go` | F1 (guards), I1 (`coverage_thresholds_l3_test.go`) | L1/L2 archtest files are not edited |

**Rebase rule.** Before opening a PR a lane rebases on `origin/main`, re-runs `make verify` and `make gate`, and checks `git diff origin/main -- openapi.json web/src/api/schema.d.ts` is empty. Each PR description lists: tests added, files outside the lane's closed set (must be none), plan addenda for X1, and measured weights of new slow packages.

**Tests first (specs carry the `// matrix:` headers of C146.6):** `l3-atlas.spec.ts` — the Atlas shows the spec's own Songs (tempo × energy), a preset switch to Family × Vocal, colour by Muse (legend entries match `list_muses` names), a filter dims non-matches, rectangle-select two points ⇒ **Add to Crate** ⇒ the Crate holds them, select several ⇒ **New Record from selection** ⇒ lands on `/records/:id?sequence=arc` with the preview, the table fallback lists the same rows,
`requested-got` shows the guide lines and an empty-state message when no pair exists; `l3-sequence.spec.ts` — preview a Record, Apply (the order changes, the changelog shows `Slot moved … (sequenced: arc)`), the Sequence tab shows the explanation and the `stale` badge after a manual reorder, an agent-proposed sequenced Record appears (through the API helper) and the human publishes; `l3-taxonomy.spec.ts` — an agent proposal (API helper) appears with its impact preview,
Accept selected applies a subset, the Song appears under the facet, Reject closes; `l3-next.spec.ts` — New shows "What next" items, a hint link opens the Muse page, an estimate hint opens `/human/generate` (the spec stops there: it never confirms spend), a hidden Muse's item disappears, the needs-you badge counts a proposed Record.
Every spec resets its own data and does not depend on counters other specs change; served-operation coverage reports zero unserved UI ops among the six; `/__e2e/leaks` is `[]`; only the specs the change affects run locally (`scripts/pw`), the matrix runs in CI.
Done: `make e2e` green (once, by this lane) or its CI shards green.

---

## Definition of done (L3)

- All named tests above green; `make verify` green in every lane; `make check`, `make test`, `make test-long` (acceptance packages change), `make perf` and `make e2e` green once at the integration lanes and in GitHub CI (chrome + firefox per the `// matrix:` headers), with zero unserved UI ops among the six L3 ops and `/__e2e/leaks` empty; `make gate-full` and `make routing` green; coverage thresholds (C146.3) met on merged data.
- Parity: every L3 op with an MCP face (6) has a parity case (REST = MCP = stdio after normalisation) and the human-judgment op has REST-only tests; the extended `propose_record` and `edit_record_slots` cases carry the `sequence` arguments; the `tools/list` golden is re-baselined under the new ceiling (C142); `TestAcceptance_M2a_AgentPerformsEveryUIAction` covers the five new UI+MCP operations and, if D-L3-22 is approved, no longer lists `map_sound_term`; the registry holds **133** operations.
- Invariants: `library verify` (L1 + L2 + L3) clean on every fixture and on the production copy; `TestProperty_FeatureInvariants` (60 per CI run, 300 weekly) green; no derived row has a second writer (`TestSQL_WriteOwners_L3`); the new tables follow the no-delete/frozen rules; without endpoints the planner never returns an order worse than its input (`Verify` F4 and `TestPlan_NeverWorse`; with endpoints the cost may rise and says so, `TestPlan_EndpointsMayCostMore`).
- Acceptance (concept §7 L3): an MCP agent builds and proposes a Record from 4★+ Picks (sequenced with an explanation) and a human publishes — `TestAcceptance_L3_AgentBuildsAndProposesRecord`; Muse-aware next actions — `TestAcceptance_L3_MuseAwareNextActions`; taxonomy proposals with human approval — `TestAcceptance_L3_TaxonomyProposalFlow`; the Atlas renders the library (mirror: `TestAcceptance_L3_AtlasRendersMirror`; real data: X2/X3).
- Privacy: the Muse-text allowlist gains exactly `list_next_actions`; every other L3 surface is ids-and-numbers only; the C144 canaries green; no real titles, artists, inspirations, lyrics, hostnames, balances or personal data in any tracked file; the public plan copy contains nothing below the private-appendix marker.
- Money: no L3 op spends; every hint names a free operation; the SPA's generation hand-off ends at the M3 estimate page.
- Scope: the M6 Song Atlas track is removed from the M6 draft and the roadmap in the same merge (X1).
- The Ears lane (E1) is not on the blocking list: L3 is complete with key null and energy predicted-only if it is cut (D-L3-35).
- Performance targets of C146.4 met on the CI runner and (Atlas, Settle, next actions) on the production copy.
- Every concept §7 L3 acceptance item maps to a green named test or a signed-off X2/X3 item; the release tag is recorded under the release convention of the day.

## Risks / open questions

- **The key and energy estimators are unproven on real music.** The key thresholds are starting values until E1 step 0 measures them; both are validated on synthetic signals only (24 harmonic triad progressions; click, pad and noise tracks). Key estimation fails on modal, modulating and percussion-dominated music; energy is a composite of onset density, brightness and mastered loudness, not perceived arousal. Mitigations: null-when-unsure for key, *relative* use of energy in sequencing, every value labelled with its basis, the Ears lane separable (D-L3-35), X2/X3 spot checks on three reference recordings. If energy ranks the operator's recordings badly, the definition is revised as `mm-energy/2` with a golden — not patched in place.
- **Predicted energy is a hand-made table.** A mood/genre/tempo prior can be wrong for a particular Song; it exists so that an unrendered library can be placed at all. It is drawn hollow, counted separately, hideable, and replaced by the measured value the moment a take exists. Once ≥ 50 Songs have *both* a prior and a measurement, calibrating the table is a data-driven revision (`mm-energy-prior/2`), noted for after X3; no learning is built now.
- **A map that is mostly guesses.** At planning time ~96 % of the library is unrendered, so the default Atlas is mostly hollow points until M3 renders and Ears measures. The totals strip, the hollow markers and the "Hide predicted points" toggle are the guard; the real default map is **a handful of dense stacks** (59 distinct tempos × a few predicted-energy values, `TestLayout_DenseStacks`), not a scatter, and the legend says predicted energy partly encodes tempo band; the requested-vs-got view is **empty by design** until the first generated takes are measured (reference recordings have no requested tempo), and says so.
- **Sequencer weights and the `arc` target are provisional.** They encode a reasonable DJ-and-album heuristic (smooth tempo, harmonic neighbours, a rise to a peak at 70 % and a soft landing), not a tested taste model. Everything is in one table (`C137.3`), changes need a decision and a golden, the explanation shows why each step is where it is, applying is an ordinary logged edit (a human can reorder), and `steady`/`ramp_up` exist as alternatives. X3 reviews the first real Record by ear.
- **Next-action noise.** Rules fire on state, there is no dismissal, and at library scale a rule such as `muse_unrendered` will list 150 Muses. Mitigations: ≤ 5 items per code plus a count, priority order, hiding a Muse silences its items, and the rule list is small and each rule is cut-able (D-L3-35). If X3 finds persistent noise, a per-Muse "snooze" is the next move (a column, not a table) — not built now (YAGNI).
- **Removing a capability from agents (D-L3-22).** `map_sound_term` was agent-writable in L1, and concept §5 lets agents edit the taxonomy. The P.5 box records the operator's explicit decision; until it is ticked the fallback is the default (agents keep it, proposals optional) and only F2's frozen `Meta`, TP2's guard and INT-B's parity edit wait. Either way proposals and their impact preview are available.
- **`list_next_actions` joins the Muse-text allowlist (D-L3-16).** It is the only new surface that names Muses; the concept already shows them to agents. If the operator prefers none, the item texts can use ids and `get_muse` hints with a one-rule change and a golden; the guard test pins whichever is chosen.
- **Atlas staleness (revised).** The token hashes the in-scope rows (C141.3), so only a change that actually alters the scope, a flag, a colour or a coordinate invalidates open pages (409 `ATLAS_STALE`); a Song outside the scope changing does not. During a large backfill the SPA would still restart repeatedly: it debounces and offers "Map changed — reload"; steady state is quiet. The cost of the token is one scope query per page (budgeted in C146.4).
- **Atlas scale.** The scope is capped at 5 000 Songs (newest first) and paged at 600 points; larger libraries need a filter. No bins or aggregation are built (D-L3-1); if a 20 000-Song library appears, a server-side density view is the next move.
- **Shared-decode refactor touches L1's tempo analyzer.** `ffmpeg.Mono` is new and `(*FFmpeg).Tempo` stays as a wrapper over it, so L1's argv and tempo tests keep their expectations; the residual risk is a regression in the one decode path, covered by `TestTempo_StillPasses`, `TestFFmpeg_MonoDecodeArgv` and the existing media-tier tests.
- **L2 is a plan, not code.** L1 and M4a are built and were verified; every name taken from L2 rev 3 (`SlotOp`, `ChangeText`, `Propose`, `recordview.GetOptions`, `FixtureL2`, the Crate vocabulary) is re-read by each lane's first commit against the merged code, and a rename is recorded in this plan in the same commit. If L2 slips, L3 does not start (F1 needs migration 0010 and the L2 tables); only L3 work that touches no L2 code may be prototyped early.
- **M6 absorption changes a plan not yet built.** Dropping the SA track and its tempo analyzer is a clean cut (L1 already ships tempo), but M5 Tier B's `tempo_ratio`/`key_relation` are no longer surfaced anywhere in L3; when M5/M6 land they add a *basis* to `features` (a derived-cache migration, cheap by construction, extension move 1).
- **One decode, 240 seconds.** The three analyzers see the first 240 s only; long Songs' later sections (key changes, tempo changes) are not measured. Documented as a blind spot in the dossier.
- **Scope valve.** If time is short, apply the ordered cut list of D-L3-35 (non-blocking, non-concept items first; the concept acceptance — Record sequencing, Muse-aware next actions, taxonomy proposals, the Atlas — is never cut). The Ears lane is separable by design; taxonomy proposals are independent of every other lane.
- **`sounds.Map` is shipped taxonomy code (D-L3-41).** The `applyTx`/`recompute` split is behaviour-preserving and guarded by the unchanged L1 `TestMap_*`, but it touches the one write path the operator uses for taxonomy edits today; TP1 lands it with the recompute hook uncalled in production until INT-B wires it, and a crash between `Decide`'s commit and its recompute is healed by the existing startup sweep.
- **Lane count and merge pressure.** Twenty-six lanes in parallel worktrees can only work because every shared file has one owner (the hot-spot table) and the API surface is frozen after F2; a lane that discovers it needs a second owner's file stops and raises a freeze fix instead of editing it. The serialised heavy-suite lock (`MM_HEAVY_SLOTS`, default 1) makes whole-repo runs queue: lanes run `make verify`, not `make test`.
- **Tier A in the e2e server is unverified.** Whether the e2e server's Ears runs the new analyzers on the fake provider's audio is confirmed by INT-W's first commit; if not, the L3 specs use draft Songs (declared tempo, predicted energy), which is enough for the Atlas, the sequence preview and next actions — and the measured paths stay covered by the media-tier Go tests.
- **Subject rule inherited from `sounds`.** A Song whose takes are all discarded and that has no draft has no `song_features` row and is not plotted (counted as `missing`); the draft's fallback to a discarded take is intentionally gone (D-L3-39). The 20/24-character timestamp ordering of L1 (`recorded_at DESC, id DESC` as a string) is inherited, not fixed, by L3.

## Revision log

### Revision 3 (2026-11-02) — as shipped: what each lane changed versus revision 2

The contracts above are the design of record; where the code differs, this log says how. All 133 operations are registered; no L3 operation spends money.

**Operator decisions.** D-L3-1, D-L3-9 and D-L3-37 are approved. D-L3-22 is **not** approved: `map_sound_term` stays open to agents (no guard, no `TestMap_AgentRefused`, no parity change). D-L3-23: **no dedicated MCP tools**; every L3 operation is reached through `call_operation` (`TestToolsList_L3Budget` asserts no L3 operation is a tool). D-L3-14 and D-L3-16 are **not** approved, so their defaults apply: the Atlas returns predicted points (hiding them is the SPA's toggle) and `list_next_actions` names Muses by a short id only, never by title. D-L3-27 (the M6 Song Atlas track is absorbed into L3) is recorded in the roadmap.

**Lanes.**

- **F1.** `synthpop` emits `electronic` before `pop`, so the primary `genre_family` of `synthpop, reggae` is electronic (the example in C133 is corrected; the rule is unchanged). `sounds.SubjectOf(ctx, Querier, songID)` is the exported subject rule. The feature literals of the fixtures are Go values, not rows, so the oracle covers value columns only. No migration `0011` collision.
- **F2.** The two taxonomy-agent tools of C142 became generic. `toolsCeilingBytes` rose to 108 300 and the L1 six-tool budget became a named constant of 16 700 (`get_song.features` is the one schema L3 adds to an L1 tool). `sequence.Service.Suggest` and `atlas.Service.Get` take no viewer: the handler resolves the page-size default. Perception and dossier `key`/`energy` are `omitempty` (absent, never null), permanently, so no golden or schema moves. Features wiring is opt-in (`L3Options.RealFeatures`, set by `serve`). The import-graph rule for `muses` is a direct-import rule plus `TestMuseReaders_L3`.
- **F3.** The Atlas is a sub-view of Library in the navigation.
- **FC1.** Integers only (BPM ×100, energy ×10 000). A discarded out-of-range requested BPM does not fall through to the next source. `Detail` is a deterministic JSON document.
- **FC2.** Digests use facet values, not `sound_state` digests, and a row is also rewritten when only its `feature_def` differs. The CLI Reconciler installs the feature step, so a CLI settle and a server settle derive identical rows; `library reconcile` prints a features count. `library verify` gained the feature, sequence and proposal anomaly families (F1..F6).
- **E1.** The key analyzer adds subharmonic chroma votes (an amendment of C136.1 step 4, part of the `mm-key/1` definition). The energy analyzer has an absolute onset floor (part of `mm-energy/1`). The shared decode is `ffmpeg.Mono`; `TierA.Tempo` was replaced by `TierA.Mono`. `analysis.KeyEnabled` is the scope-valve constant for key. The measured key and energy are on perception and the dossier.
- **SQ1/SQ2.** New reason codes `tempo_shift`, `key_diagonal`, `tempo_unknown` and `energy_unknown`. The planner is exact up to 12 Songs (Held-Karp) and a beam plus local search above; `TestPlan_NeverWorse` holds without endpoints, and with endpoints the cost may rise and says so.
- **SR.** A Record pin is a Take pin, so pinned slots take part in the plan and keep pin and side. New 422 codes `SEQUENCE_ENDPOINTS_CONFLICT`, `SEQUENCE_SOURCE`, `SEQUENCE_TOO_SMALL`; a repeated Song id is `UNKNOWN_SONG`. A `stale` run is also one whose slot now resolves to another Take. The `proposed` change row carries `{sequence_id, strategy}`.
- **NA1/NA2/NA3.** Evidence keys starting with `_` are rule-private. Muse text uses the last 8 characters of the id (the first 8 of a UUIDv7 are a timestamp prefix and look alike). The reader pool does not enforce read-only, so the GET-never-mutates rule is pinned by tests. `taxonomy_unmapped` has the style word as its subject. A Song-scoped call restricts through `SongScope`.
- **TP1/TP2.** `Map` is split into `applyTx` and `recompute`; the recompute hook (`features.ComputeTx`) runs inside every batch. An already-effective `add` is dropped but not reported (no field in the frozen API). `Impact.UnmappedCleared` is derived from the same parse as the gain and loss. `Propose` computes the impact inside its write transaction. `decide_taxonomy_proposal` is a human act on `/api/` with a runtime check (a paired device may decide). An expired proposal stores the system actor and no note.
- **AT1/AT2.** Layout is integer and deterministic; the spiral formula implies a stack of 100 identical points spans about four cells, which the test pins. The Atlas reads state and Record colour in single passes in Go (guarded by `TestAtlas_StateEqualsSongState`; if C90.4 changes, both change). Axis ranges are fixed-point.
- **AD.** `get_adherence` and `get_song.features`. The unit is a live Song whose subject is a take; error is truncated per comparison; `octave` is the stored flag.
- **WM1/WM2.** A point carries only its position; tooltips and the table read values back from it (approximate for stacked points). "Estimated" in the totals strip is a lower bound. The page honours `?near=`, `?muse=` and the filter query.
- **WA1/WA2/WS.** Proposals tab, What next lists with hint links, needs-you counts, Song and Muse entry points, key and energy chips, and the Sequence tab on the Record studio.
- **I1.** Coverage floors are measured, not targets; weekly property jobs; shard weights.
- **INT-B.** The recompute hook was already live under `RealFeatures`; INT-B pins it. The parity rig runs with L3 and real features and compares populated answers.
- **INT-W.** Playwright specs for the Atlas, proposals, next actions, sequencing and entry points. Bugs fixed: the hidden-predicted note, SPA navigation into the Atlas, hint links on Song and Muse lists, and the Records list reading `?status=`.

**After integration.**

- Draft-subject Songs got no `song_features` row, which `library verify` reported as `song_features_missing`; fixed in a follow-up.
- The measured key is not yet trusted on real music: the detector's correlation on real takes is good but there is no ground truth, and multi-part imports of one song get different keys. The operator decides at X3 whether to keep the measured key or set `analysis.KeyEnabled = false` (the runbook describes the fallback).
- Known gaps, not built: the Atlas point carries no tempo, key or energy values (an API change after L3); key and energy badges on the Track page; the Atlas has no Board angle filter.
